# --- Travel photos ---
# Intro paragraphs shown above the photo grid on the Elsewhere tab (rendered as separate <p> tags).
elsewhere_intro:
  - >
    I moved around quite a bit growing up, and later got to travel professionally as a
    flight attendant, right before grad school. I didn't plan any of it, but looking back,
    getting to see so much of the world turned out to be one of the best things that ever
    happened to me, and I'm forever grateful for that. The best part was always the people,
    and I made some genuinely lifelong friends along the way, through the most random
    events and coincidences, the kind that still check in on me years later.
  - >
    Somewhere along the way I also became a hopeless photo hoarder. I've learned to live
    with the iCloud "storage full" warning, my phone is constantly cluttered, but I still
    snap a photo of pretty much every moment and love looking back at them later. I barely
    exist on social media, so most of these have only ever lived on my phone, until now.
  - >
    These days I try to fly less, largely out of concern for its environmental impact.
    Climate change feels undeniably tangible now, and it's something I think about a lot
    these days. It's heartbreaking to see how much some places have changed, they don't
    feel the same. Below are some of those moments I hoarded along the way.

# Add real photos by dropping the file in assets/img/travel/ and adding

# --- Off the clock ---
# Reverse-chronological list of dated blurbs shown on the "Off the clock" tab.
now_entries:
  - date: August 2026
    text: >
      Been on a bit of a movie kick lately. The Odyssey was really good, and I finally
      caught the new Spider Man too (still not sure how I feel about it). Watched Nowhere
      Special on a whim and cried hysterically. Absolutely wrecked me, in the best way.
  - date: Jan 2026
    text: >
      Watching a lot of police body cam footage and listening to live ATC audio lately,
      don't ask why. Somehow I find it interesting, though I've spent way more time on
      it than I probably should.
  - date: Nov 2025
    text: >
      Reading a lot of speculative fiction between deadlines, trying (and mostly failing)
      to keep a sourdough starter alive, and slowly working through my list of national
      parks — still need to actually write that list down properly.
---

<div class="misc-page">

  <section id="misc-panel-elsewhere" class="misc-panel active">
    {%- for para in page.elsewhere_intro %}
    <p class="misc-intro">{{ para }}</p>
    {%- endfor %}

    <div id="misc-globe" class="misc-globe" data-points="{{ page.globe_points | jsonify | escape }}"></div>
    <p class="misc-globe-hint">Drag to spin, click a pin to open that place's photos.</p>

    <div class="misc-travel-grid" id="misc-travel-grid" style="display: none;">
      {%- for photo in page.travel_photos %}
      <div class="misc-photo-card {% if photo.tall %}tall{% endif %}"
        onclick="openGallery('{{ photo.tag }}')" onkeydown="if(event.key==='Enter'){openGallery('{{ photo.tag }}')}" role="button" tabindex="0">
        <div class="misc-photo">
          {%- if photo.img %}
            <img src="{{ photo.img | relative_url }}" class="img-fluid rounded z-depth-1" alt="{{ photo.place }}" loading="lazy" decoding="async" />
          {%- else %}
            <div class="misc-placeholder {{ photo.color }}" data-tag="{{ photo.tag }}"></div>
          {%- endif %}
        </div>
        <div class="misc-caption">
          <div class="misc-place">{{ photo.place }}</div>
        </div>
      </div>
      {%- endfor %}
    </div>

    {%- for photo in page.travel_photos %}
    <div id="gallery-{{ photo.tag }}" class="misc-lightbox">
      <div class="misc-lightbox-inner">
        <button type="button" class="misc-lightbox-close" onclick="closeGallery('{{ photo.tag }}')" aria-label="Close">&times;</button>
        {%- if photo.gallery %}
        <div class="misc-lightbox-grid">
          {%- for gphoto in photo.gallery %}
          <div class="misc-lightbox-photo">
            <img src="{{ gphoto.img | relative_url }}" class="img-fluid rounded z-depth-1" alt="{{ gphoto.alt }}" loading="lazy" decoding="async" />
          </div>
          {%- endfor %}
        </div>
        {%- elsif photo.img %}
        <div class="misc-lightbox-single">
          <img src="{{ photo.img | relative_url }}" class="img-fluid rounded z-depth-1" alt="{{ photo.place }}" loading="lazy" decoding="async" />
        </div>
        {%- else %}
        <div class="misc-lightbox-placeholder {{ photo.color }}"></div>
        <p class="misc-lightbox-soon">Photos still to come for this one.</p>
        {%- endif %}
        <p class="misc-lightbox-place">{{ photo.place }}</p>
      </div>
    </div>
    {%- endfor %}
  </section>

  <section id="misc-panel-clock" class="misc-panel">
    <div class="misc-now-list">
      {%- for entry in page.now_entries %}
      <div class="misc-now-entry">
        <div class="misc-now-date">{{ entry.date }}</div>
        <p class="misc-now-text">{{ entry.text }}</p>
      </div>
      {%- endfor %}
    </div>
  </section>

</div>

<style>
  .misc-page {
    font-family: 'Lato', sans-serif;
    color: var(--global-text-color);
  }

  .misc-panel { display: none; }
  .misc-panel.active { display: block; }

  .misc-section-label {
    font-size: 0.75rem;
    letter-spacing: 0.08em;
    text-transform: uppercase;
    color: var(--global-theme-color);
    margin: 0 0 0.75rem;
  }

  .misc-intro {
    font-size: 0.98rem;
    line-height: 1.75;
    color: var(--global-text-color-light);
    max-width: 640px;
    margin: 0 0 1.1rem;
  }
  .misc-intro:last-of-type {
    margin-bottom: 2.2rem;
  }

  /* 3D globe */
  .misc-globe {
    width: 100%;
    height: 420px;
    margin-bottom: 0.4rem;
    border-radius: 6px;
    overflow: hidden;
    background: var(--global-card-bg-color);
    border: 1px solid var(--global-divider-color);
  }
  .misc-globe canvas { cursor: grab; }
  .misc-globe:active canvas { cursor: grabbing; }
  .misc-globe-fallback {
    display: flex;
    align-items: center;
    justify-content: center;
    height: 100%;
    font-size: 0.85rem;
    color: var(--global-text-color-light);
    text-align: center;
    padding: 1rem;
  }
  .misc-globe-hint {
    font-size: 0.75rem;
    color: var(--global-text-color-light);
    margin: 0.6rem 0 2.2rem;
  }

  /* travel grid */
  .misc-travel-grid {
    display: grid;
    grid-template-columns: repeat(3, 1fr);
    gap: 1.4rem;
    margin-bottom: 1rem;
  }
  @media (max-width: 768px) {
    .misc-travel-grid { grid-template-columns: repeat(2, 1fr); }
  }
  @media (max-width: 480px) {
    .misc-travel-grid { grid-template-columns: 1fr; }
  }
  .misc-photo-card { display: flex; flex-direction: column; }
  .misc-photo-card.tall { grid-row: span 2; }
  .misc-photo { width: 100%; aspect-ratio: 4 / 3.2; overflow: hidden; border-radius: 4px; }
  .misc-photo img { width: 100%; height: 100%; object-fit: cover; display: block; }

  .misc-placeholder {
    width: 100%;
    border-radius: 4px;
    aspect-ratio: 4 / 3.2;
    position: relative;
    overflow: hidden;
    box-shadow: 0 1px 4px rgba(0,0,0,.08);
  }
  .misc-photo-card.tall .misc-placeholder { aspect-ratio: 4 / 9.4; }

  .misc-placeholder::after {
    content: attr(data-tag);
    position: absolute;
    inset: auto 10px 10px auto;
    font-size: 0.65rem;
    letter-spacing: 0.05em;
    text-transform: uppercase;
    color: rgba(255,255,255,.85);
    background: rgba(0,0,0,.28);
    padding: 3px 8px;
    border-radius: 20px;
  }

  .misc-placeholder.p1 { background: linear-gradient(135deg,#d7c3a1,#a8895f); }
  .misc-placeholder.p2 { background: linear-gradient(135deg,#c9d6d3,#7fa39a); }
  .misc-placeholder.p3 { background: linear-gradient(135deg,#e3cdd1,#b98a94); }
  .misc-placeholder.p4 { background: linear-gradient(135deg,#cdd3e0,#8b98b8); }
  .misc-placeholder.p5 { background: linear-gradient(135deg,#e6dcc3,#c2a86a); }
  .misc-placeholder.p6 { background: linear-gradient(135deg,#d3ded0,#8fac86); }

  .misc-caption { margin-top: 0.6rem; min-height: 2.6em; display: flex; align-items: flex-start; }
  .misc-place {
    font-size: 0.85rem;
    font-weight: 700;
    color: var(--global-text-color);
    display: -webkit-box;
    -webkit-line-clamp: 2;
    -webkit-box-orient: vertical;
    overflow: hidden;
    line-height: 1.3;
  }
  .misc-note { font-size: 0.8rem; color: var(--global-text-color-light); line-height: 1.5; margin-top: 0.15rem; }

  /* every card opens a lightbox on click */
  .misc-photo-card { cursor: pointer; }
  .misc-photo-card:focus-visible { outline: 2px solid var(--global-theme-color); outline-offset: 3px; }
  .misc-photo { position: relative; }

  .misc-lightbox {
    display: none;
    position: fixed;
    inset: 0;
    background: rgba(0,0,0,.82);
    z-index: 1000;
    padding: 4rem 1.5rem;
    overflow-y: auto;
  }
  .misc-lightbox.active { display: block; }
  .misc-lightbox-inner {
    max-width: 900px;
    margin: 0 auto;
    background: var(--global-bg-color);
    border-radius: 6px;
    padding: 2rem;
    position: relative;
  }
  .misc-lightbox-close {
    position: absolute;
    top: 0.7rem;
    right: 1rem;
    background: none;
    border: none;
    cursor: pointer;
    font-size: 1.9rem;
    line-height: 1;
    color: var(--global-text-color-light);
  }
  .misc-lightbox-close:hover { color: var(--global-text-color); }
  .misc-lightbox-grid {
    display: grid;
    grid-template-columns: repeat(3, 1fr);
    gap: 0.8rem;
    margin-top: 1.2rem;
  }
  @media (max-width: 768px) {
    .misc-lightbox-grid { grid-template-columns: repeat(2, 1fr); }
  }
  .misc-lightbox-photo img {
    width: 100%;
    aspect-ratio: 1 / 1;
    object-fit: cover;
    display: block;
  }
  .misc-lightbox-single img {
    width: 100%;
    max-height: 60vh;
    object-fit: cover;
    display: block;
    border-radius: 4px;
    margin-top: 1.2rem;
  }
  .misc-lightbox-placeholder {
    width: 100%;
    aspect-ratio: 16 / 9;
    border-radius: 4px;
    margin-top: 1.2rem;
  }
  .misc-lightbox-placeholder.p1 { background: linear-gradient(135deg,#d7c3a1,#a8895f); }
  .misc-lightbox-placeholder.p2 { background: linear-gradient(135deg,#c9d6d3,#7fa39a); }
  .misc-lightbox-placeholder.p3 { background: linear-gradient(135deg,#e3cdd1,#b98a94); }
  .misc-lightbox-placeholder.p4 { background: linear-gradient(135deg,#cdd3e0,#8b98b8); }
  .misc-lightbox-placeholder.p5 { background: linear-gradient(135deg,#e6dcc3,#c2a86a); }
  .misc-lightbox-placeholder.p6 { background: linear-gradient(135deg,#d3ded0,#8fac86); }
  .misc-lightbox-soon {
    font-size: 0.8rem;
    color: var(--global-text-color-light);
    margin: 0.6rem 0 0;
  }
  .misc-lightbox-place {
    font-size: 0.85rem;
    font-weight: 700;
    color: var(--global-text-color);
    margin: 1.3rem 0 0;
  }
  .misc-lightbox-caption {
    font-size: 0.92rem;
    font-style: italic;
    color: var(--global-text-color-light);
    line-height: 1.6;
    margin: 0.3rem 0 0;
  }
  .misc-lightbox-caption:first-of-type { margin-top: 1.3rem; }

  /* off the clock */
  .misc-now-list {
    display: flex;
    flex-direction: column;
    gap: 1.6rem;
    max-width: 640px;
  }
  .misc-now-entry {
    border-bottom: 1px solid var(--global-divider-color);
    padding-bottom: 1.5rem;
  }
  .misc-now-entry:last-child {
    border-bottom: none;
    padding-bottom: 0;
  }
  .misc-now-date {
    font-size: 0.72rem;
    letter-spacing: 0.06em;
    text-transform: uppercase;
    color: var(--global-text-color-light);
    margin-bottom: 0.4rem;
  }
  .misc-now-text { font-size: 1rem; line-height: 1.7; margin: 0; color: var(--global-text-color); }
</style>

<script src="https://unpkg.com/globe.gl"></script>
<script>
  // Switches the visible panel to `target` ('elsewhere' or 'clock').
  // Tabs are gone from the page itself now — switching happens entirely
  // through the "misc" nav dropdown's #elsewhere / #clock links.
  function selectMiscTab(target) {
    document.querySelectorAll('.misc-panel').forEach(function (p) {
      p.classList.toggle('active', p.id === 'misc-panel-' + target);
    });
  }

  // Reads the current #elsewhere / #clock hash and shows that panel.
  function applyMiscTabFromHash() {
    var hash = (window.location.hash || '').replace('#', '');
    if (hash === 'elsewhere' || hash === 'clock') {
      selectMiscTab(hash);
    }
  }

  document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded', function () {
    initMiscGlobe();
    shuffleTravelGrid();

    // Land on the right tab when arriving via the nav dropdown, and keep
    // switching correctly if the dropdown is used again without a full
    // page reload (clicking #elsewhere while already on #clock, etc.).
    applyMiscTabFromHash();
    window.addEventListener('hashchange', applyMiscTabFromHash);

    // close a lightbox when clicking its dark backdrop, or on Escape
    document.querySelectorAll('.misc-lightbox').forEach(function (lb) {
      lb.addEventListener('click', function (e) {
        if (e.target === lb) {
          lb.classList.remove('active');
          document.body.style.overflow = '';
        }
      });
    });
    document.addEventListener('keydown', function (e) {
      if (e.key === 'Escape') {
        document.querySelectorAll('.misc-lightbox.active').forEach(function (lb) {
          lb.classList.remove('active');
        });
        document.body.style.overflow = '';
      }
    });
  });

  // Reshuffle the travel photo grid into a new random order on every page
  // load, so it's a different mix each time instead of always alphabetical.
  function shuffleTravelGrid() {
    var grid = document.querySelector('.misc-travel-grid');
    if (!grid) return;
    var cards = Array.prototype.slice.call(grid.children);
    for (var i = cards.length - 1; i > 0; i--) {
      var j = Math.floor(Math.random() * (i + 1));
      var tmp = cards[i]; cards[i] = cards[j]; cards[j] = tmp;
    }
    var frag = document.createDocumentFragment();
    cards.forEach(function (c) { frag.appendChild(c); });
    grid.appendChild(frag);
  }

  function openGallery(tag) {
    var el = document.getElementById('gallery-' + tag);
    if (el) {
      el.classList.add('active');
      document.body.style.overflow = 'hidden';
    }
  }
  function closeGallery(tag) {
    var el = document.getElementById('gallery-' + tag);
    if (el) {
      el.classList.remove('active');
      document.body.style.overflow = '';
    }
  }

  // 3D globe of everywhere pinned above the photo grid. Wrapped defensively:
  // if the CDN script didn't load (offline, blocked, etc.) this quietly
  // shows a text fallback instead of breaking the rest of the page.
  function initMiscGlobe() {
    var container = document.getElementById('misc-globe');
    if (!container) return;

    var points;
    try {
      points = JSON.parse(container.getAttribute('data-points') || '[]').filter(function (p) {
        return typeof p.lat === 'number' && typeof p.lng === 'number';
      });
    } catch (e) {
      points = [];
    }

    if (typeof Globe === 'undefined' || !points.length) {
      container.innerHTML = '<div class="misc-globe-fallback">Interactive globe couldn\'t load (needs an internet connection) — use the list below instead.</div>';
      showTravelGridFallback();
      return;
    }

    try {
      var world = Globe()(container)
        .globeImageUrl('https://unpkg.com/three-globe/example/img/earth-blue-marble.jpg')
        .bumpImageUrl('https://unpkg.com/three-globe/example/img/earth-topology.png')
        .backgroundColor('rgba(0,0,0,0)')
        .pointsData(points)
        .pointLat('lat')
        .pointLng('lng')
        .pointColor(function () { return '#B8860B'; })
        .pointAltitude(0.025)
        .pointRadius(0.85)
        .pointResolution(24)
        .pointLabel(function (p) { return p.place; })
        .onPointClick(function (p) { openGallery(p.tag); })
        .onPointHover(function (p) { container.style.cursor = p ? 'pointer' : 'grab'; })
        .width(container.clientWidth)
        .height(container.clientHeight);

      world.controls().autoRotate = true;
      world.controls().autoRotateSpeed = 1.4;
      world.controls().enableZoom = true;

      // Pause the auto-rotation while hovering so clustered pins (e.g. Europe)
      // hold still long enough to aim for. Scroll/pinch to zoom in also helps
      // spread tightly-packed pins apart.
      container.addEventListener('mouseenter', function () {
        world.controls().autoRotate = false;
      });
      container.addEventListener('mouseleave', function () {
        world.controls().autoRotate = true;
      });

      window.addEventListener('resize', function () {
        world.width(container.clientWidth);
        world.height(container.clientHeight);
      });
    } catch (e) {
      container.innerHTML = '<div class="misc-globe-fallback">Interactive globe couldn\'t load — use the list below instead.</div>';
      showTravelGridFallback();
    }
  }

  // If the globe can't render at all (no internet, blocked CDN, etc.),
  // show the photo grid by default instead of leaving it hidden with no
  // way in.
  function showTravelGridFallback() {
    var grid = document.getElementById('misc-travel-grid');
    if (grid) grid.style.display = '';
  }
</script>
